Second transcription's note: Derived from the return-level Schedule C presence and statutory-employee gate, plus the clergy helper; the supplied interface does not separately supply all Schedule SE self-employment situations.

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?" The candidate adds s1.sc_1a (statutory employee) and its constant-no eic.clergy to the Schedule C test; statutory-employee and clergy income are refused by scope.schedule1, so the readings coincide. If you meet these requirements, make sure you check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"; i1040gi p. 41, Step 3, question 3: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?" The candidate tests filing status only (its note: "actual married status is not separately supplied"), so an unmarried head of household gets the box; household.married is in the published interface and the project reads it (eic.married_rules). Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 47, Worksheet B: "Use this worksheet if you answered “Yes” to Step 5, question 2."; Part 4, line 5: "No. STOP You can't take the credit." The candidate shows line 4b (a copy of Step 5 earned income) on every return, including filers who never reach Step 5 or answer No to Step 5, question 2, and refuses every self-employed filer; the project shows 4b only on eic.show_wsb4b (BUG-0156: Step 5 reached and Step 5, question 2 Yes). Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.deepseek.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 126 partial records; 1,725 comparisons): 491 of 1,725 agree; 905 candidate-shows-skipped (418 on completed returns), 297 candidate-refuses (127 on completed returns), 21 candidate-unknown, 11 project-refuses. Witness: classm.f2441.2.deepseek (completed; no Schedule C, Step 1 question 1 STOP) hidden, candidate $85,000.00. The candidate refuses every Worksheet B filer (its eic.wsb_4b refuses on eic.self_employed, note: "The self-employment, farm and statutory-employee parts of Worksheet B are not supplied by the closed interface"), but the published interface carries person.eic_se, Worksheet B Parts 1 and 2 per person, which the Claude reviewer used; the project computes Worksheet B. A refusal of a computation the source defines and the interface supports is a model error, not a scope screen. Witness: classm.f5695.5 (completed). The candidate-unknown are incomplete records (ss.multi, ss.base.below) where the project hides the line without reading the withheld answer. The 11 project-refuses are the cited scope.schedule1 gate (stage12.refuse.tips_trade_or_business), scope. BUG-0156 check: eic.show_wsb4b requires eic.reached_step5, so 4b is hidden for a filer who does not reach Step 5, and no project-side difference on 4b occurs on a completed return. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." The candidate figures line 6 on every return and reads lines 2 and 5, so it inherits their unknowns; the project shows it only when Steps 1 to 5 pass. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.deepseek.json; 1,099 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 126 partial records; 1,725 comparisons): 826 of 1,725 agree; 830 candidate-unknown (349 on completed returns), 24 candidate-shows-skipped (11 on completed returns), 11 value, 34 candidate-refuses. Witnesses: stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn (completed; Step 1 question 2 STOP) hidden, candidate $649.00; classm.f2441.2.deepseek candidate unknown. The value differences on returns the project completes or leaves incomplete are all Worksheet B filers (eic.self_employed true): the project's printed line 6 also carries Worksheet B line 11 (cite: "Worksheet A lines 2 to 6 are Worksheet B lines 7 to 11"), which the candidate's Worksheet A line cannot express; interface gap, as in round 2. The value differences on refused returns are Worksheet B values or lines whose shown_when itself refuses or is unknown (classm.sc.1: eic.eligible refuses on person[spouse].f8889[hsa].excess), which triage counts as shown; no amount is filed (scope). Witnesses: classm.sli.4 project $0.00, candidate $503.00; stage14.eic.wsb_loss $614.00 against $649.00. The refusals are on refused returns (scope).

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.self_employed, household.credit_disallowed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.self_employed |
| eic.check_27c | eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.self_employed |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.other_income | - |
| (Step 5, 3) | scope.other_income | - |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | - |
| 4b | eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.self_employed |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | eic.self_employed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | eic.self_employed |
| 1 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | - |
| 2 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | - |
| 4 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    note: "The clergy and church-employee substitutions described in Step 5 line 1 are not supplied by the closed input interface; the supported case copies Form 1040 line 1z."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "The supplied interface has no Medicaid waiver payment amount; the supported case assumes none, and scope.other_income refuses an out-of-scope return rather than filling an absent amount with zero."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "The supplied interface has no nontaxable combat pay election amount; the supported case excludes any combat pay election by refusing s8812.combat_pay."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
    refuses: [eic.self_employed]
    note: "The self-employment, farm and statutory-employee parts of Worksheet B are not supplied by the closed interface; the supported no-self-employment case has line 4b equal to line 4a, the Step 5 earned income."

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.h_wsb_4b_pos}

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t0

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If the amount on line 3 is not less than the threshold, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t0
    note: "The table amount is computed even when the worksheet directs the filer to leave line 5 blank; line 6 selects line 2 in those cases."

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_used, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_line6_smaller
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_used, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line2

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [s1.sc_1a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}
    note: "Derived from the return-level Schedule C presence and statutory-employee gate, plus the clergy helper; the supplied interface does not separately supply all Schedule SE self-employment situations."

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_credit_computed
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line2_zero_stop, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_zero_stop, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses on page 1 of Form 1040 or 1040-SR."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Uses filing status MFS or HOH as the supplied proxy for the Step 3 question about a married taxpayer; actual married status is not separately supplied."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or Christian Science practitioner with Schedule SE earnings also reported on Form 1040 line 1z."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: true}, {const: false}]}
    note: "The closed input interface does not supply clergy or church-employee status or the Schedule SE overlap amount; the supported slice assumes no clergy adjustment."

  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children with valid SSNs."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_line2_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line2_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_thr0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 5 threshold, no qualifying children, not married filing jointly."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}

  - id: eic.h_thr0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 5 threshold, no qualifying children, married filing jointly."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_thr1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 5 threshold, one or more qualifying children, not married filing jointly."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}

  - id: eic.h_thr1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 5 threshold, one or more qualifying children, married filing jointly."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable Worksheet A line 5 threshold."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ne: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr1_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ne: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr1_other

  - id: eic.h_line5_used
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Whether Worksheet A line 5 is used on line 6."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_line6_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet A lines 5 and 2."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line2, eic.line5]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_4b_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 4b, floored at zero."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 6, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 6, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 6, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 6, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7 EIC Table credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t0

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8 amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Whether Worksheet B lines 8 and 6 are the same."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line8, eic.wsb_6]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 8, no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.h_wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 8, one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.h_wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 8, two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.h_wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table credit for line 8, three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.h_wsb_line8, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 10 EIC Table credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t0

  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_used
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Whether Worksheet B line 10 is used on line 11."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line9, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_wsb_line8, eic.h_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet B lines 10 and 7."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.h_wsb_line7, eic.h_wsb_line10]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11 earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line10_used, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_smaller
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line10_used, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7

  - id: eic.h_credit_computed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit before the line 27c STOP adjustment."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_credit
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line6

  - id: eic.h_line2_zero_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A line 2 zero STOP."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_zero_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7 zero STOP."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line7, {const: 0}]}
```
